| `lem:exposed-facts-wide-tree` | For any exposed set B_e of tail rows and the labeling y_P of the residual exposed rows (assigned rows keep their labels, unassigned rows of a cut last node's term at or after its round get a label of the pattern, all other unassigned rows get the last round's trigger hole): (a) every round's host is consistent with zeta_y on B_e except rounds charged to a light exposed row moved inconsistently at the round's node, at most \|R_e\| of them; (b) a consistent host with a matched pair has no round with its reduced tail, and no round at all when its rows outside B_e are matched consistently | Working proof; on the round-first order fact (a) fails (the pinned shared-cube reader) | [Lemma X](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#wide-rounds-exposed-facts) |
| `thm:round-bounds-on-wide-tree` | The few-light-rows theorem (4n^(-c-2)), the combined theorem and its size corollary (6n^(-c-2)), the two-role few-rows theorem (4n^(-c-2)), the two-role exposed theorem and its Corollary 2 (6n^(-c-2)) and the two-role Corollary 1 (3n^(-c-1)) hold on the reordered wide-round tree, hence every round bound of clauses (a) to (g) of `thm:current-term-composition`'s corollary; their proofs use of the tree only the two facts (`lem:exposed-facts-wide-tree`), Lemma 1 (`lem:wide-rounds-transfer`) and the 1-leaf rule, with the charged rounds absorbed as in Lemma 2 of the complete-term rule | Working proof (each proof re-read); with wide rows, clauses (b), (e), (g) add nothing beyond (a), (d), their J_0 capping the total width | [Theorem T](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#wide-rounds-exposed-transfer) |
| `lem:few-wide-rows` | nu(G) <= \|R' n B_w\|, and Pr[\|R' n B_w\| >= r] <= (e b_w (N+1)/(r(n+1)))^r <= n^(-eps r) for at most n^(1-eps)/N^4 wide rows; so such a reader has fewer than r_0 wide queries on every path except with probability n^(-c-2), whatever its wide patterns and size | Working proof (one line from `lem:wide-cover-bound`) | [Lemma Y](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#wide-rounds-exposed-corollaries) |
| `cor:few-tail-rows-any-width` | A role-separated reader, or a two-role reader under sparse pinning, with at most n^(1-eps)/N^4 tail rows (light and wide together), at most n^c terms for any c, light patterns in the light range 2^(w+4) s <= e <= n/(32 r N^4 (N+1)) and wide patterns of any width, switches under a uniform rho' in Phi_0 within the height budget except with probability 4n^(-c-1), for N and s as stated and n large | Working proof (`thm:round-bounds-on-wide-tree`, `lem:few-wide-rows`, `thm:wide-round-composition`); the first switching statement of the window at every polynomial size with tail rows of every width | [Corollary A](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#wide-rounds-exposed-corollaries) |
| `cor:few-wide-rows-below-n2` | A role-separated reader, or a two-role reader under sparse pinning, of size n^c, 1 <= c < 2 - eps, with at most n^(1-eps)/N^4 wide rows, any number of light rows in the light range and wide patterns of any width, switches except with probability 7n^(-c-1); against `cor:split-rows-below-n2` it reaches 2 - eps in place of 2 - 2 eps and needs no wide mass condition, at the price of few wide rows | Working proof | [Corollary B](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#wide-rounds-exposed-corollaries) |
| `lem:wide-matching-rows` | Lemma W2 and its polynomial form hold with row degrees d_r(x) = #(wide rows j with x in S_j), S_j the union of the cubes of the patterns on j, and the row mass sum_j \|S_j\| in place of the wide degree and wide mass; the recorded proof uses the degree only to count the rows adjacent to a hole | Working proof | [Lemma W2 (rows)](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#wide-mass-rows) |
| `thm:single-row-wide-readers` | Every pin-free reader of single tail literals [j in p], of any widths and in any number, switches under a uniform rho' in Phi_0 within the height budget except with probability e^(-N) + n^(-c') (2n^(-c-1) with N >= (c+1) ln n), the wide-round tree having height at most 2^d + t - 2: row mass at least 4N(n+1) forces a satisfied term at the root by negative association over the matched rows, and below it Lemma W2 by rows bounds the wide matching number; contains the shared-cube reader at every size, the unary readers, and the reader of every pattern of l-1 literals on every row | Working proof; census of the wide-round tree on the all-patterns reader (satisfied at the root in every restriction) | [Theorem V](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#wide-mass-theorem), [scope](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#wide-mass-scope), [checks](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#wide-mass-remaining) |
| `rem:wide-cover-in-code` | Theorem W holds with the cover of any decoder-independent graph containing the queried edges, in particular the alive wide graph (incidences of terms not falsified at the root), written into the code: at most (2N+2)^(nu_1) values, and the exponent becomes -(5N/48) log2 N + (2r+1)(2 + 3 log2 N) + 1 <= -N/8 + (2r+2)(2 + 3 log2 N) for N >= 3 | Remark with the count; not implemented in the checker | [Remark](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#wide-mass-pinned) |
| `obs:pinned-wide-mass-barrier` | For terms [a -> y] and [j in p] the count of Lemma W2 on the alive incidences of light pins bounds the heavy holes only when the light pins' total row mass is at most n^(3-2 eps)/(N+1), i.e. c < 2 - 2 eps, because it charges every light pin while only one pin per pinned row is matched; the repair is a dichotomy on the matched row mass, large mass giving a satisfied term by negative association over the wide rows given the pinned images, and its missing step is the concentration of that mass, and of its part outside the pinned labels, over the uniform injection of the pinned rows (Azuma over the sequential exposure); multi-row wide terms need the alive graph (the reader [j in p] and [j' = z] with \|C(p)\| = n/2 has nu(G) about N/2 but few alive incidences) | Obstruction and route, with the missing step named; line parked at `conj:literal-frontier`; a Monte Carlo control of the matched-mass mechanism is recorded as a finite check in the [route review](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#route-review-check) (2026-09-18) | [Analysis](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#wide-mass-pinned), [remaining](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#wide-mass-remaining) |
| `conj:literal-frontier` | Conjecture L: every reader of at most n^c terms of at most r pins and pattern literals of any width, role-separated or two-role, switches under a uniform rho' in Phi_0 within height N/8 - r except with probability O(n^(-c-1)), for a polylogarithmic N; the general claim of the literal line of cycles 175 to 214, with its proved and open cases; no recorded obstruction contradicts it; parked, because it bears on the main goal only if terms with dense forms are handled by a method that composes with it | Conjecture; parked by the route review | [Conjecture L](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#route-review-conjecture), [what the goal needs](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#route-review-need) |
| `q:dense-forms-compact-family` | Question D: on the compact family a form becomes an affine form on the residual bit space V of dimension (N+1) l_2, with linear part fixed by (Q, R') and constant fixed by mu' (zero for equal row functionals when l_2 >= 2); open are a distribution lemma for the constants, whether a wide reader of dense-form terms covers V, and a covering certificate of degree within N/8; structured families (equal row functionals, tribes on V, the fresh-block sources) are the first falsification targets; sparse forms (row support at most n^(1-eps)/(N+1)) reduce to boundedly many residual rows and do not meet the obstruction | Open question selected as next step; heuristics unproved; answered for generic sources by `lem:dense-forms-invariance`, `lem:dense-forms-covering-certificate` and `obs:dense-forms-balanced-rank` (2026-09-18) | [Alternatives and Question D](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#route-review-alternatives) |
| `lem:dense-forms-invariance` | For independent uniform forms L_i = sum_j <a_j, x_j> + c_i, the restrictions under every fixed rho' in Phi_0 are independent uniform affine forms on the residual bit space V of dimension v = (N+1) l_2; hence a random dense reader restricts to a random reader of the same shape on F_2^v; the same holds for one-bit parities over uniform row sets with N+1 variables | Working proof; exact rank check of the restriction map for l = 3 over every flat and residual row set | [Lemma I](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#dense-forms-invariance), [checks](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#dense-forms-checks) |
| `lem:dense-forms-restricted-rank-packing` | A reader whose restricted linear parts span dimension m is a function of m forms and has an exact image with all certificates through degree 2m + w from Booleanity alone; readers on forms with the same functional on every row are functions of the sum of the residual labels (m <= l_2), class-symmetric forms have m <= l_2 min(g, N+1); sparse forms reduce to boundedly many residual rows | Working proof | [Lemma P](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#dense-forms-structured) |
| `lem:dense-forms-covering-certificate` | a >= 2^w (D_w ln 2 + ln(1/eta)) independent random terms of rank w on F_2^v span the multilinear polynomials of degree at most w except with probability eta; then 1 is an F_2-combination of the terms, the reader is identically 1, and P-hat = 0 has all certificates through degree w; for a = n^c generic dense terms this holds after the restriction up to rank about c log2 n / log2(2v+2), against rank below c before it | Working proof for random sources; 40-trial span checks at (v,w) = (8,2), (10,2), (10,3) | [Lemma C and corollary](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#dense-forms-certificate), [checks](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#dense-forms-checks) |
| `obs:dense-forms-balanced-rank` | Coverage of two distinct points by a random term of rank w is exactly independent, so the uncovered share of V concentrates at (1 - 2^-w)^a; at rank about log2 a the restricted generic reader is balanced and, by invariance, distributed as a generic reader on F_2^v: the coverage hypothesis of Question D fails there and the restriction gains nothing; open are fixed structured sources in low rank, certificates between the certified and the balanced rank, and the balanced rank itself | Working proof (Lemma B) and obstruction for generic sources; the core of the general step on the compact family; the PHP-relative escape shows no gain on small boards, see `check:hole-axioms-dense-form-images` (2026-09-18) | [Lemma B and table](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#dense-forms-balanced), [remaining](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#dense-forms-remaining) |
| `check:hole-axioms-dense-form-images` | Exact degree-d polynomial-calculus closures V_d of the row axioms over F_2 on boards with 4, 6 and 8 holes, without (labelings) and with the hole exclusions (functional PHP); refutation degree 3, 4 and above 4; for blocks on dense forms (flats of codimension 2 and 3 on uniform half-sets or affine half-spaces; readers of 2, 4 and 8 rank-two dense terms at s = w = 2 on eight holes with the degree-4 closure, dim V_4 = 143643 of 241993) the linear image conditions are feasible for the same parameters with and without the hole axioms, while a block pinning two rows to one hole is feasible at s = 2 only with them; the four-hole test proposed in the dense-forms entry is vacuous | Finite check with a positive control; not a theorem | [Results](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#php-relative-images-results), [method](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#php-relative-images-method), [scope](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#php-relative-images-meaning) |
| `lem:block-substitution-bound` | Polynomial-calculus form of the recorded prefix-product substitution (cycle 170): r_1 = 1, r_j = prod_{i<j}(1 + L_i) sends P to prod_j(1 + L_j) and the companions to zero modulo Booleanity and row exclusions, and maps a degree-d derivation to a base derivation of degree at most d + rho - 2 (lines kept multilinear in the new variables), fixing old polynomials; rho <= 2 fully conservative (the recorded `cor:affine-rank-two-same-degree-conservativity`), rho = 3, 4 not refutable through D_0 - 2, D_0 - 3; uses only g^2 = g and Booleanity with row exclusions | Working proof; restatement of a recorded substitution | [Lemma](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#augmented-php-setup) |
| `check:augmented-php-degree-small-boards` | Exact degree-d PC closures of the functional PHP over F_2 augmented by accuracy-one blocks on dense forms: on eight holes at degree four (refutation degree five) blocks with 3 inputs (uniform half-sets, affine half-spaces, two seeds), 4 inputs, and two blocks of 3 inputs do not put 1 in the closure; for the blocks with 3 and 4 inputs the old-only part of the closure has the dimension 143643 of the base closure, so every base design of degree four extends, although the substitution bound certifies this only through degree three (two for 4 inputs); six holes at degree three is conservative in all cases (weak test); controls: rho = 2 is conservative as proved, a fake non-conservative axiom raises the old-only dimension | Finite check of low power (one degree of slack); an instance on the PHP base of `conj:level-one-fresh-block-conservativity`; the exact block forms have no image at s <= 2 and V_4 cap T_3 = V_3 on eight holes | [Table](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#augmented-php-results), [reading](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#augmented-php-meaning) |
| `obs:level-two-plain-elimination-out-of-reach` | On the functional PHP with N holes closure tests are nonvacuous only below degree N/2 + 1; pure second-level companions have degree five (the form companion of a mixed block: four) and the recorded rank-three loss sits at degree seven, which needs N >= 14 and about 1.2e11 old monomials (1.6e7 at N = 10, d = 5, where pure companions enter unmultiplied and the mixed form companion with one multiplier of degree one; 1.3e9 at N = 12, d = 6); plain elimination with generic dense blocks cannot reach the recorded loss; symmetry, the quotient by the base closure and small non-PHP unsatisfiable bases are not covered, the last being the remaining exact test | Feasibility count; confirms with numbers the remark of cycle 169 | [Count](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#level-two-feasibility-count) |
| `ctx:pc-extension-variables-literature` | Imported context: Impagliazzo-Mouli-Pitassi (CCC 2023) bound PC size over F_p with M extension variables of arity O(log n); over F_2 the question reduces to the degree of the augmented system by size-degree tradeoffs; Dahiya-Mahajan-Mouli (ECCC TR23-132) reach N^(1+eps(1-delta)) extension variables of arity N^(1-eps) and size N^c for an XOR-ified generalized PHP, by restrictions that shrink extension axioms plus cleanup; both restrict extension variables to depend on original variables only, and IMP identify the recursive case with AC0[p]-Frege; DMM note that constant-depth PC also simulates CP, SOS, AC0[q]-Frege and TC0-Frege, so general bounds for it are harder than the goal; the general step (dense, nested extension variables on ordinary PHP) lies beyond both | Imported statements (abstracts, IMP Theorems 1 and 2 and Section 3 remark, DMM Theorem 1.2 and proof outline read; proofs not read) | [Literature and position](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#level-two-literature), [source audit](https://github.com/kbr-/math-research/blob/main/research/notes/SOURCE_AUDIT.md) |
| `obs:collapse-induction-stops-at-frontier` | The level-by-level collapse of wide low-rank generic readers is the recorded removal of an input span containing one, applied to the images of the inputs (cost zero; exact image 1 + sum c_i q_i whenever it annihilates every input); together with clamping and packing it leaves exactly the frontier of `obs:middle-rank-frontier` and `cor:polylog-high-threshold-cube-volume`: generic readers with term rank between about c log n / log log n and the high threshold, including the balanced rank; the induction does not close and the proposal of the previous entry is withdrawn as a route | Composition of recorded results; no new lemma | [Collapse step](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#collapse-induction-composition), [survivors](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#collapse-induction-survivors) |
| `lem:term-value-case-split` | PC form over F_2 of the recorded case split of `audit:adaptive-query-fixed-distribution-gap`: if F + {Z} and F + {1+Z} both have PC refutations of degree B for a Boolean-valued Z of degree w <= B, then F has one of degree B + w; so for any a flats some sign vector keeps F hard through D_0 - 1 - a w (additive loss, the recorded rank-control obligation) | Restatement of a recorded lemma; only the PC proof and the term-value phrasing are new | [Lemma S](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#term-value-clamping) |
| `q:generic-term-value-clamping` | Reduction: if PHP + {Z_c + e_c} has no degree-B refutation for some values e_c of the first-level flat indicators of rank at most w, a pure source reading only those products has none of degree B / max(1, w-1); the source must be pure (inputs are F_2-affine combinations of earlier products); extends the constant-value profiles of `lem:clamped-bottom-zero-source-profile` from weight-supplied zero clamps to arbitrary constants under a hardness hypothesis; Question H: do independent generic flats below the covering threshold a ln(1/(1-2^-w)) = v ln 2, with a margin (and v - a of order N for w = 1), leave PHP_N hard through N/8, and near and above it is there always a cheap covering certificate or a hard sign vector? Not given by weights (count), restrictions (Lemma I; constant forms on a sub-board only for about log n / log log n forms) or the case split; not a statement about literal terms | Reduction proved; question open | [Reduction and question](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#term-value-clamping) |
| `check:generic-axiom-tipping-counts` | Exact degree-d PC closures over F_2 with random dense axioms added one at a time, on the functional PHP (six and eight holes), the satisfiable bijective board and labelings: the first count at which 1 enters, for parity constraints and rank-two terms; closure increments are seed-independent generic ranks until one saturation jump, which on the PHP is the tipping (quotient 36 to 4953 left before it) and on the controls leads to the vanishing ideal of the surviving models at a comparable count; the PHP never tipped before the jump of the bijective control or of the labelings with affine forms, and tipped earlier (factor about 0.7 to 0.85 after normalisation) than the labelings with uniform forms; random signs survived 14 to 16 parity constraints at degree three on eight holes where the case split guarantees one; counts are governed by quotient over multipliers and say nothing about degree N/8 | Finite check of low power (ranks one and two, degrees two and three) | [Design, table and reading](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#term-avoidance-finite-check) |
| `conj:image-or-value` | Claim G, the general claim of the dense-forms line: for a source with n^c first-level flats there are value axioms E (linear equations in the bits or term values Z_c = e_c, chosen from the source) such that the base with E has no PC refutation of degree B and modulo E every block has an old image of degree s certified by the base and E; literal switching (Conjecture L) and generic term values (Question H) are its extreme cases; the compact bit base is itself a system of avoidance axioms for the collision flats, of total density about n/2, below the generic covering threshold | Formulation stated by a route review for compiled sources; open, not asserted; candidate counterexample for arbitrary sources: the union of a wide literal reader and the row-mixing twisted approximate-majority source (incompatible axioms); hardness part recorded only for structured E (labels confined to one flat, matching restrictions) | [Claim](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#dense-review-claim), [need](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#dense-review-need), [estimate and selection](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#dense-review-alternatives) |
| `obs:twisted-approximate-majority-values` | The approximate-majority source of `thm:universal-Boolean-source-map-obstruction` (cycle 149) has, for any fixed set of at most 0.3 v coordinates, a circuit of the recorded form every term of which meets the complement I; modulo E = {u_i = 0, i in I} all its blocks have constant images (factor t-1 once). In the original coordinates, or under one affine bijection applied to every label, take the same 0.3 l bit positions in every row: the base with E is the compact base with n+1 rows and n^0.3 holes, hard through n^0.3/2 by `lem:compact-bit-ordinary-PC-decoder` and `cor:functional-auxiliary-PC-bridge`, so the recorded obstruction source satisfies Claim G. Under a generic row-mixing affine twist (p = 2, same barrier) it does exactly if the compact base has no low-degree refutation on a generic affine subspace of codimension about 0.7 v; this cannot be moved to the residual board | Working reduction; original coordinates and row-by-row twists settled with recorded hardness, the row-mixing case open | [Test](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#dense-review-falsification) |
| `cor:safe-linear-systems-keep-base-hard` | Corollary SL: if a linear system over F_2 in the label bits is safe of rank a (one pivot in each of a distinct rows; Efremenko-Garlik-Itsykson) with 4(a-1) < n, every PC refutation of the functional unary base with the decoded system adjoined, hence of the compact base with the system, has degree at least n/2 - a + 1, for every right-hand side; corollary of `lem:top-row-linear-monomial-detector` by weighted replay with the indicator of the solution space (derivation due to the reviewer); a direct unary restriction leaves the functional PHP with n+1-a rows and n-2a holes; several pivots per row: `lem:multi-axis-cube-residual-detector` gives (n-F)/2 - (a-u) + 1; a uniformly random system of a <= n/4 equations is safe except with probability a 2^{-(m-a+1)l}; loss per equation equals the recorded case split | Working proof (corollary of a recorded detector); does not reach rank above the number of blocks | [Corollary and proof](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#safe-systems-lemma) |
| `conj:generic-subspace-refutation-degree` | Conjecture H1: for a uniformly random affine subspace W of the bit space the functional unary base with the decoded equations of W has PC refutation degree at least c min(n, dim W / l); elementary upper bound ceil(dim W / l) + O(1) in unary degree (projection to r rows with r l >= dim W + t is injective except with probability 2^-t); data: v - a* at tipping is 6 (eight holes) and 7 (sixteen holes) at degree two and 12 at degree three on eight holes, which does not decide the scaling, and the bijective control tips at the same free dimension at degree two | Conjecture with three data points of low power; needed at dim W = 0.3 v for the row-mixing twist of `obs:twisted-approximate-majority-values` | [Table and conjecture](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#parity-subspace-scaling), [what remains](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#parity-subspace-remaining) |
| `ctx:php-linear-constraints-literature` | Imported context: Miksa-Nordstrom Theorem 4.9 (graph functional PHP over an (s, delta)-boundary expander of left degree d needs PC degree > delta s/(2d)) would give at most n/(2d) for value axioms confining each row to its own flat if the flat graph expands (unchecked), transfer from the bit encoding not written out; Byramji-Impagliazzo (after Efremenko-Garlik-Itsykson): safe systems and closure for linear systems on the bit PHP, estimates for rank r <= cn; general Res(parity) described as open beyond depth N^(2-eps) | Imported statements (arXiv versions, proofs not read) | [Literature](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#parity-subspace-literature), [source audit](https://github.com/kbr-/math-research/blob/main/research/notes/SOURCE_AUDIT.md) |
| `lem:residual-coordinates-linear-elimination` | Lemma R: over F_2, for independent consistent affine equations Lambda in the label bits with zero flat W and any injective affine parametrization P of W, the PC refutation bit degree of the compact base with Lambda equals max(l, degree of the collision-flat system {E_ii' o P} in the residual coordinates); the PHP on W is the statement that the collision flats of n+1 affine maps cover F_2^(dim W) | Working proof, a composition of `lem:affine-coordinate-completion`, the substitution replay and the Boolean certificate of affine forms; Nullstellensatz dual (odd set meeting subflats evenly) recorded as a remark | [Lemma R](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#subspace-residual-lemma) |
| `check:subspace-residual-bit-degrees` | Exact PC closures in residual coordinates for uniform subspaces (three seeds): largest refuted dimension at bit degree d is 9, 12, 16 on sixteen holes (d = 4, 5, 6) and 11, 15 on thirty-two (d = 5, 6), equal to the generic dimension count or one to two below it, with the bijective control within one; on eight holes 7 and 10 to 11 at d = 3, 4, and every subspace at d = 5 because the full base has bit degree exactly five (four holes: three) | Finite check; it does not test Conjecture H1, whose bit form is implied by the trivial bound l for dim W <= 2 l^2 (only dim W <= 17 was reached, where the control is also unsatisfiable); it tests the stronger hypothesis that the degree stays within two of the generic count; tool research/tools/subspace_flat_cover_closure.cpp | [Table and reading](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#subspace-residual-check) |
| `obs:subspace-design-attempt-obstructions` | A nonzero weight vanishing outside a subspace of codimension a has bit degree at least a, hence unary degree at least a/l, above the base barrier at codimension 0.7 v; a uniform subspace of codimension a contains no two points differing in at most r rows except with probability C(n+1,r) 2^(r l - a), hence no product of label sets with two points, so the recorded tensor-product designs (signed cubes, row trades) have no support inside it, while the affine restriction behind Corollary SL reserves two holes per equation | Working observations from the failed proof attempt at Conjecture H1; weights with f Lambda_c in the old ideal only through the working degree are not excluded; Conjecture H1 parked untested next to Conjecture L | [Attempt](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#subspace-residual-attempt), [decision](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#subspace-residual-remaining) |
| `lem:parity-tree-bounds-bit-degree` | Lemma T: over F_2, a parity decision tree of depth d >= l for the collision search on an affine subspace W gives a Nullstellensatz certificate, hence a PC refutation, of the collision-flat system of bit degree at most d; D_b(W) <= max(l, depth) and <= max(l, dim W) | Working proof; sharpens the l + d obtained from `lem:term-value-case-split`; uses only that the axioms are flat indicators | [Lemma T](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#wall-review-wall) |
| `cor:compact-bit-base-degree-order-n` | The compact bit base has PC bit degree between n/2+1 (recorded) and 2n-2+l (halving decision tree), and every affine subspace W has D_b(W) <= min(max(l, dim W), 2n-2+l); exact values 3 and 5 on four and eight holes | Working corollary of Lemma T; settles order n against order n l | [Corollary](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#wall-review-wall) |
| `cor:compact-family-generic-subspace-incompatible` | Corollary U: for a uniform affine subspace W of codimension a >= (N+1) l + log2 C(n+1,N+1) + t, except with probability 2^(-t) every restriction of the compact family leaves at most one point of W; on the union source the restriction for the literal reader and the generic equations for the twisted source cannot be combined | Working corollary of `obs:subspace-design-attempt-obstructions`; Claim G itself is not refuted | [Corollary U](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#wall-review-falsification) |
| `ctx:route-review-subspace-wall` | Route review: the value method's hardness inputs, the union source and the unary parities of the simplest recorded unexcluded class (one OR over parities of PHP variables at middle rank) all need PHP hardness under linear constraints of rank of order v, while recorded and published techniques stop at rank of order n; the extension-variable methods of Dahiya-Mahajan-Mouli and Impagliazzo-Mouli-Pitassi need unnested definitions, (M + mn) kappa k = o(n^2), a formula surviving weight-pk assignments and the loss of kappa holes per round, an XOR lift, and p > 2 | Review with imported context; estimate for the value-method line about 5 percent; selects the parity-decision-tree depth on a generic subspace for at most two entries | [Review](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#entry-2026-09-18-route-review-subspace-wall) |

Does not refute a source with one common old root. | [Full statement and proof](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#proper-row-subfamilies-no-global-gluing) |
| `thm:tail-signature-compact-composition` | Recording each hidden filling row's tail-pattern signature removes the tail-free filling reservoir from the compact current-term decoder, charging K per move and giving the stated conditional switching bound. | All tail rows allowed. Retains own-row sparse pinning, explicit population/richness bounds, and a separately proved heavy-round bound p_R on the same compact tree. A_K=2rK N(N+1)e/(n-N-e+1); failure <=p_R+Mr exp(-s)+A_K^s(2N(N+2))^nu0. Exact averaging over all filling sets removes the old 7/5 factor. General K can be n even with one-literal patterns; no general source exclusion. | [Statement and complete proof](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#entry-2026-09-20-tail-signature-composition) |

This refutes only the unmodified code after dropping its tail-free filling hypothesis, not the historical decoder in its proved scope. | [Statement and complete proof](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#entry-2026-09-20-tail-signature-composition) |

Common-prefix pin status is fixed; tail trace polynomials are triangular and degree-bounded. The augmented assignment includes explicit nonslot filling labels and omits still-unassigned round-query rows. Distinct preimages have distinct slot-label vectors. No efficient unique decoder asserted. | [Full statement, proof and controls](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#entry-2026-09-20-compact-trace-list-rank) |

A_0=2rN(N+1)e/(n-N-e+1), s=floor(N/12)-2r>=nu0=floor(N/48)>=1. W=(s+2)^(2s+nu), L=sum_{a<=min(s ell,rk(2s+nu))}binom(s ell,a). A separate compact round bound remains required. Predicates have F2 indicator degree <=k and density >=beta. | `check:duplicate-block-filtered-closures` | Exact GF(2) closures distinguish an NS-only helper saving at rank three from a genuine degree-six PC interface gain at rank four for accuracy-two copies. | Finite Boolean-domain controls: at rank three and ceilings 5,6 the NS ranks are 6,80 and PC ranks 6,382; P+Q enters PC only at six and enters neither NS space. At rank four they are 8,144 for both proof systems, with P+Q absent throughout. Degree-six PC closure has eight eligible basis rows at rank four, versus fifty at rank three. The checker preserves original degree-five generator activity, uses descending-degree echelon columns, independently verifies closure and exports separating duals for absent targets. The fresh accuracy-one helper identity is verified separately through six. Positive rank-three membership has no separately exported derivation trace. Complete reports and reproduction instructions are retained; the general symbolic theorem does not rely on finite extrapolation. | [Mixed-accuracy affine helper and filtered closures](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#duplicate-block-filtered-closures) |
| `thm:certificate-forest-supported-lifting` | A complete ENS certificate admitting an ancestor-compatible coefficient-support forest lifts a prescribed old NS design with supported-lift costs along forest paths, at most p^q D for height q. | Working all-prime certificate-specific theorem. Inputs of each block use only old variables and strict ancestor coefficients; each companion-cofactor monomial together with its own block lies on one forest chain. Old-axiom and field-equation multiples have no support restriction. Starting with an old normalized design through the recursively computed B, lift each root block through E=max(D,child budgets) using the historical ordinary supported lemma, then glue child functionals sharing this exact enlarged-base marginal by zero projections and subtraction of duplicate base terms. The final functional preserves the old degree-D marginal and annihilates all stated supported multiples, not arbitrary cross-branch companion multiples or the full augmented PC closure. The exact budget is Phi_a(max(D,child B)); monotonicity and Phi_a(E)<=pE give p^qD. For common h,delta it is max(D,C+p^q(D-C)) with C=[ph+((p-1)h-(p-2))delta]/(p-1). Forest height concerns the actual certificate, not merely structural source depth. | [Certificate forest and normalization budget](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#certificate-forest-supported-lifting) |
| `cor:PHP-certificate-forest-exclusion` | Functional unary PHP excludes degree-D NS certificates with the stated compatible forest whenever N>=2B-1; B<=p^qD allows polylog D and q<=(1-epsilon)log_p N. | Working conditional endpoint on the complete functional unary PHP board with m=N+1. Apply the old all-field normalized matching design at the exact forest budget B, then the certificate-specific lift. Weak-base certificates inherit the exclusion by viewing them over the stronger functional base. No claim is made that a Frege compiler certificate has a forest in this range, or that arbitrary PC proofs inherit the same graph restriction. Arbitrary total inventory is permitted only under the explicit input-ancestor and coefficient-chain conditions. | [Certificate forest and normalization budget](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#PHP-certificate-forest-exclusion) |
| `obs:short-factor-unbounded-interaction-depth` | One old-affine level and bounded monomial block support do not bound coefficient-interaction forest height; even 2h affine cofactor factors can force a K_(t,t) complete-diagonal graph. | Working analytic scope controls. The historical consecutive-prefix certificate has a path on M blocks, minimum forest height ceil(log2(M+1)) and binary affine supported budget Theta(Mh) at D=4h+2. A cofactor product over h row sums in each of two t-block families has degree 2h and only 2h affine factors, but every pair of complete selected-column diagonals occurs with coefficient one. Its complete-diagonal graph contains K_(t,t), whose minimum forest height is t+1. This is a polynomial-format control, not a PHP refutation or a lower bound on all equivalent certificate representations. It prevents inferring the forest hypothesis solely from short factored cofactors or bounded complete support per monomial. | [Certificate forest and normalization budget](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#short-factor-unbounded-interaction-depth) |
| `audit:compiler-forest-hypothesis-gap` | The recorded outer-cofactor compiler does not supply the compatible low-height forest hypothesis: two MP multipliers permit dense named interactions, and MOD names retain unhandled definitions. | Source-interface audit with an exact local MP identity, not an impossibility theorem for PHP certificates. Unrolling two MP steps produces multiplier b_1 b_2 on an inner contribution. Binary MOD values can be sums of two disjoint retained-name families, yielding a K_(t,t) named support graph at outer weighted cost at most 2L. This does not prove that every collected concrete witness preserves that graph, or that an alternative representation is impossible. The named-value compiler replaces those expressions with variables only while retaining their costed defining equations, which are outside the proved ENS forest-lifting rule. Hence short affine factors and fixed source depth do not establish the forest application. The line is parked pending an actual cancellation or representation theorem; the PHP normalization obligation remains open. | [Compiler forest audit and decision](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#compiler-forest-hypothesis-gap) |

| `prop:general-cover-from-zero-avoiding-triples` | If for each fixed odd q, zero-avoiding triple covers need n(q-1)+omega(1) hyperplanes, then the conjectured f_q(n,k,1) value holds for every fixed k>=2 at sufficiently large n. | Working sufficient reduction; the divergence premise remains unproved and is not claimed necessary. | [Complete statement and proof](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/branches/odd-field-double-covering/#general-cover-from-zero-avoiding-triples) |
